# Commands · S · part 4

1,469 entries, sdlc-release to security-audit-follow-up-seeds. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **sdlc-release**: Run the production-release step — promote staging → main after UAT, dispatch the release-engineer for versioning + changelog + prod deploy config, tag, and deploy (Gate ④). Refuses while a sprint is Active. ([danniel-isiah-libor/danniel-claude-plugins](https://github.com/danniel-isiah-libor/danniel-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude-sdlc-team/commands/sdlc-release.md) · ★2)
- **sdlc-release**: G9 · branch, commits, PR as submitted, qa-handoff.md, then gate. After G8; every change class. ([fxblog-guru/sdlc](https://github.com/fxblog-guru/sdlc/blob/HEAD/commands/sdlc-release.md) · ★1)
- **sdlc-release-check**: Release readiness: evaluate the go/no-go criteria, assemble the release package list, check deployment ordering and the rehearsed rollback, and produce the go/no-go record. Usage: /sdlc-release-check [version] ([kaitondt/ai-pack](https://github.com/kaitondt/ai-pack/blob/HEAD/packs/sdlc-standard/.claude/commands/sdlc-release-check.md) · ★1)

- **sdlc-review**: Run ONLY the QA code-quality loop (review → fix → re-review until clean or the round cap) without re-running the implementation lifecycle. With a story key → reviews its fix/ or feat/&lt;STORY-KEY&gt; branch (whichever exists) against the plan + ACs. With no key → Diff mode: reviews the current working changes vs develop. Runs the QA Engineer playbook inline. Not for implementing. ([whimzyLive/nightshift-ai](https://github.com/whimzyLive/nightshift-ai/blob/HEAD/docs/reference/commands/sdlc-review.md) · ★3)
- **sdlc-review**: Run the whole review phase in one command — verify the build once, fan out five specialist lenses in parallel, then merge, deduplicate, fix mechanically, and sign off. Works inside a feature workspace or standalone on a diff, branch, or PR. ([DevLab-Technologies/claude-sdlc](https://github.com/DevLab-Technologies/claude-sdlc/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-pipeline/commands/sdlc-review.md) · ★2)

- **sdlc-review-fix**: Ingest review comments from a GitHub PR or commit, triage them with the receiving-code-review skill (keep only the ones true in this app's context), fix the accepted ones via the right domain agents, re-review the applied commits, then post each comment's accept/reject justification back on its PR thread: resolving accepted threads and leaving rejected ones open. Runs the QA Engineer playbook inline. Does not re-implement or open a PR. - **sdlc-trace-check**: Check the traceability register: orphan detection in both directions, V-model coverage per level, and the phase-gate traceability assertions. Run before declaring any phase complete. Usage: /sdlc-trace-check [phase, module id, or nothing for everything] ([kaitondt/ai-pack](https://github.com/kaitondt/ai-pack/blob/HEAD/packs/sdlc-standard/.claude/commands/sdlc-trace-check.md) · ★1)

- **sdp-be-dto**: Use when you need request or response payload contracts at a backend boundary, when a payload reaches a use case unvalidated, or when an internal entity leaks out the API ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/sdp-be-dto.md) · 2 copies)

- **sdp-be-usecase**: Use when a backend operation spans services/repos, handler logic grows fat, or a transaction needs one owner ([devgmotta/portfolio-analytics](https://github.com/devgmotta/portfolio-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sdp-be-usecase.md) · 2 copies)
